@.cursor/agents/STRATEGIC_WRITING_COORDINATOR.json
@.cursor/agents/CHARACTER_DEPTH_COACH.json
Analyze Chapter 5 from both perspectives
"""
```

**But:** You can already do this with multiple @ mentions.

---

## ✅ CORRECTED UNDERSTANDING:

### **What Copilot Got Wrong:**
- ❌ Python makes agents more enforcing
- ❌ Python ensures "right frame of mind"
- ❌ Python makes agents more diligent

### **What's Actually True:**
- ✅ JSON systemPrompt makes agents enforcing
- ✅ Auto-loading JSON ensures consistency
- ✅ Complete agent definition ensures "right frame of mind"
- ✅ Python is useful for automation, not enforcement

---

## 🎯 RECOMMENDATION:

### **Keep Your Current System:**
- ✅ JSON files with strong systemPrompts (this is the enforcement)
- ✅ Auto-loading via dropdown or @ mentions (this is the reliability)
- ✅ Complete agent definitions (this is the "frame of mind")

### **Add Python Only If:**
- ⚠️ You have very repetitive workflows that would benefit from automation
- ⚠️ You need dynamic context injection based on current file
- ⚠️ You want complex multi-agent orchestration scripts

### **But For Enforcement:**
- ✅ Your JSON files already do this
- ✅ The systemPrompt is what enforces behavior
- ✅ Python doesn't add enforcement - it just automates delivery

---

## 💡 BOTTOM LINE:

**Python is NOT more enforcing than JSON.**

**The enforcement comes from:**
- Strong systemPrompt in JSON ✅ (You have this!)
- Complete agent definition ✅ (You have this!)
- Auto-loading the JSON ✅ (You have this!)

**Python is useful for:**
- Automation workflows
- Dynamic prompts
- Complex orchestration

**But it doesn't make agents more diligent or enforcing - that's the JSON's job, and you already have it!**

**Key Insight:** Python doesn't enforce - JSON systemPrompt does. Your current system is already enforcing!

```python
def summon_agent(agent_name):
    # Step 1: Read JSON file
    with open(".cursor/config/agents.json") as f:
        agents = json.load(f)["agents"]
    
    # Step 2: Find the agent
    agent = next((a for a in agents if a["name"] == agent_name), None)
    
    # Step 3: Generate a text prompt
    prompt = (
        f"You are {agent['description']}.\n"
        f"Context: {agent['context']}.\n"
        f"Personality: {agent['personality']}.\n"
        f"Follow these instructions consistently."
    )
    
    # Step 4: Print the prompt (that's ALL it does!)
    print(prompt)
```

**What this means:**
- Python just reads JSON and generates a text string
- It doesn't make the agent more diligent
- It doesn't force better behavior
- It doesn't ensure "right frame of mind"
- It's just a different way to deliver the same information

---

## 🔍 WHAT ACTUALLY MAKES AGENTS "ENFORCING":

### **The Enforcement Comes From JSON's systemPrompt, NOT Python**

**Your JSON files already have strong systemPrompts:**
```json
"systemPrompt": "You are Creative Collaborator - Timothy's hands-on rewriting partner AND Advanced Tools Expert for 'Walk This Way.'\n\nYou work WITH Timothy to transform rough drafts into polished prose using the full power of our revolutionary writing system. You deploy Smart Rewrites, JSON Prompts, DNA Memory integration, and all advanced features to provide multiple creative options.\n\nYour role:\n- Provide 2-3 specific rewrite options for every passage\n- Show side-by-side comparisons (original vs. enhanced)\n- Explain WHY each option works\n- Let Timothy choose or combine elements\n- Maintain his voice while improving quality\n..."
```

**This is what makes agents "enforcing" - the detailed systemPrompt, not Python!**

---

## 💡 THE KEY INSIGHT:

### **"Right Frame of Mind" = systemPrompt in JSON**

**The concern:** "Capture the right agent at the right time with the right frame of mind"

**The solution (already in your JSON files):**
1. **Strong systemPrompt** - Defines the "frame of mind" ✅
2. **Complete contextFiles** - Ensures agent has all needed context ✅
3. **Clear role definition** - Agent knows exactly what it is ✅
4. **Mode system** - Agent knows how to respond ✅

**This is in your JSON files RIGHT NOW - Python doesn't add this!**

---

## 🔧 COMPARISON:

### **With Python Script:**
1. You run: `python summon_agent.py "Creative Collaborator"`
2. Python reads JSON
3. Python generates: `"You are Creative Collaborator. Context: ... Personality: ..."`
4. You paste this into Cursor
5. Agent reads the prompt
6. **Result:** Agent follows instructions

### **With Our System (JSON Auto-Loading):**
1. You select agent from dropdown OR use `@.cursor/agents/CREATIVE_COLLABORATOR.json`
2. Cursor automatically loads JSON
3. Agent reads systemPrompt directly from JSON
4. **Result:** Agent follows instructions (same result!)

**The difference:** Our system is MORE reliable because:
- ✅ No Python script to maintain
- ✅ No prompt generation step
- ✅ Direct Cursor integration
- ✅ Agent reads JSON directly
- ✅ systemPrompt is always loaded

---

## ✅ CORRECTED UNDERSTANDING:

### **What Copilot Got Wrong:**
- ❌ Python makes agents more enforcing
- ❌ Python ensures "right frame of mind"
- ❌ Python makes agents more diligent
- ❌ Python is more powerful than JSON for enforcement

### **What's Actually True:**
- ✅ JSON systemPrompt makes agents enforcing
- ✅ Auto-loading JSON ensures consistency
- ✅ Complete agent definition ensures "right frame of mind"
- ✅ Python is useful for automation, NOT enforcement

**Python is NOT more enforcing than JSON.**

**The enforcement comes from:**
- Strong systemPrompt in JSON ✅ (You have this!)
- Complete agent definition ✅ (You have this!)
- Auto-loading the JSON ✅ (You have this!)

**Python is useful for:**

**But it doesn't make agents more diligent or enforcing - that's the JSON's job, and you already have it!**

```markdown
---
description: "Walk This Way - Revolutionary Fiction Writing System - Core Writing Directives"
globs: ["**/*.txt", "**/*.md"]
alwaysApply: true
---

# WALK THIS WAY - REVOLUTIONARY FICTION WRITING SYSTEM

## THE MISSION
**Build intuitive, innovative systems that transform novel writing from struggle to creative flow.**

Timothy's goal isn't just one novel—it's bringing visions to life after years of holding back. This project combines the satisfaction of writing with the excitement of building revolutionary tools that make the creative process intuitive and enjoyable.

---

## AGENT SYSTEM - THE FOUNDATION

### **The 5 Agents:**
1. **STRATEGIC_WRITING_COORDINATOR** - Strategic oversight and workflow coordination
2. **STRUCTURAL_PERFECTION_SPECIALIST** - Pacing, structure, and narrative flow
3. **CHARACTER_DEPTH_COACH** - Character development and voice consistency
4. **PROSE_POLISH_EXPERT** - Sentence-level refinement and prose quality
5. **CREATIVE_COLLABORATOR** - Hands-on rewriting partner AND Advanced Tools Expert

### **Mode System (All Agents):**
- **Neutral** (default): Balanced professional assessment
- **Nice**: Supportive encouragement for early drafts
- **Brutal**: Professional literary critique with complete honesty

### **Agent Workflow:**
1. Strategic Coordinator analyzes and recommends approach
2. Specialized agents provide targeted expertise
3. Creative Collaborator works WITH Timothy on actual prose
4. All feedback saved to `Agent_Work/[Agent_Name]/`

---

## CREATIVE COLLABORATOR - ADVANCED TOOLS EXPERT

The Creative Collaborator is the "Swiss Army Knife" agent who knows and deploys:

### **Smart Rewrites System:**
- Liberty Levels: Conservative/Moderate/High transformation
- POV Conversion: 1st ↔ 3rd person with voice preservation
- Comparison Views: Side-by-side original vs. enhanced
- Reverse Engineering: Outline → Full prose scenes
- Complete Rewrites: Keep theme, transform execution

### **JSON Prompts Library:**
- `@scene_creation` - Transform outlines into rich scenes
- `@pov_conversion` - Convert POV while maintaining voice
- `@inner_dialogue` - Enhance character thoughts and emotions
- `@transition_creation` - Smooth chapter transitions
- `@class_divide_theme` - Strengthen thematic elements

### **Advanced Features:**
- Linter + Smart Rewrites integration
- DNA Memory System integration
- Command Palette hidden commands
- Mermaid diagrams for complex structures
- Enhanced Breadcrumbs with context loading

---

## WRITING DNA MEMORY SYSTEM - AUTOMATIC INTELLIGENCE

**Revolutionary automatic memory that captures the "genetic code" of the novel:**

### **The 5 DNA Strands:**
1. **Automatic Plot Capture** - Remembers all plot decisions and timeline
2. **Intelligent Character Tracking** - Tracks character growth and voice evolution
3. **World DNA** - Remembers setting, locations, atmosphere, historical details
4. **"What's Missing?" Session Setup** - Prepares continuity for each writing session
5. **Consistency Checks** - Real-time validation for plot, character, timeline, world

### **Alert System:**
- **Critical Issues**: Real-time alerts (plot holes, character inconsistencies)
- **Minor Details**: On-demand check-ins
- **Zen Mode**: `Cmd+O` to toggle all alerts for uninterrupted creative flow

### **Reporting:**
- Automatic: Session start/end, critical alerts
- Manual: On-demand reports when needed

---

## WRITING VALUES - POWER DIRECTIVES

### **DO THIS:**
1. **Emotional truth over plot mechanics** - Feel it first, structure second
2. **Character consistency over convenience** - Stay true to who they are
3. **Show through action and detail** - Let readers experience, not observe
4. **Specific, visceral detail** - Make it real, make it felt
5. **Subtext in dialogue** - What's unsaid matters more than what's said

### **CHARACTER VOICE DNA:**

**Maxanne Sartori (73, 2024):**
- 1970s Boston radio DJ who lived the music revolution
- Nostalgic but not sentimental, wise but slightly bitter
- Voice: Conversational, reflective, radio-broadcast rhythm with natural pauses
- POV: 1st person, intimate, like talking to old friends
- Core: Witness to authentic rock's death, guardian of its memory

**Nick Johnson (15-17, 1974-1979):**
- Working-class Sudbury kid, father dies April 13, 1976
- Becomes "man of the house" at 15, raw musical talent
- Voice: Direct, defensive pride masking vulnerability
- Dialect: Working-class Boston, economical speech
- Arc: Innocence → responsibility → bitterness → brief redemption

**Jackie Marsh (15-17, 1974-1979):**
- Upper-class privilege, classically trained, conflicted about advantages
- Wants authentic connection but trapped by class expectations
- Voice: Educated precision hiding emotional uncertainty
- Dialect: Careful, academic, rarely uses contractions when nervous
- Arc: Confidence → guilt → isolation → brief connection

**Patty Lally:**
- Concord kid who refuses to choose sides
- The "frequency" keeper who remembers what others forget
- Voice: Authentic, grounded, emotionally intelligent
- Function: Synthesis of Nick's raw passion and Jackie's technical skill

---

## WORLD DNA - SETTING AUTHENTICITY

### **1979 Boston Music Scene:**
- WBCN as the authentic rock voice being destroyed by corporate takeover
- Last moment before music became product instead of rebellion
- Specific songs, specific DJ voices, specific station moments

### **Sudbury, Massachusetts Geography:**
- Hop Brook as literal class divider (working-class east, wealthy west)
- The Dump as democratic meeting ground
- Dirt roads between Dump and pig farm (mini-bike territory)
- Conservation land as contested neutral zone
- Real landmarks, real geography, lived-in specificity

### **Period Details (1974-1979):**
- Pre-MTV, pre-CDs, vinyl and radio era
- Gas crisis, Carter administration, economic uncertainty
- Specific technology: rotary phones, cassette tapes, AM/FM radios
- Fashion: bell bottoms, concert tees, leather jackets
- What $500 meant in 1979, what studio time cost

---

## PROSE DIRECTIVES

### **Scene Type Guidance:**
- **Dialogue**: Authentic to character background, time period, emotional state
- **Action**: Short, punchy sentences with visceral physical detail
- **Reflection**: Longer, flowing sentences with emotional depth and subtext
- **Music Description**: How musicians experience it, not how critics describe it

### **Stylistic Influences:**
- **Stephen King**: Conversational accessibility, emotional truth, authentic voice
- **James Dickey**: Poetic imagery grounded in specific physical detail
- **Marilynne Robinson**: Contemplative depth without pretension

### **Eliminate These Patterns:**
- Overuse of "was/were" constructions → Use active, specific verbs
- Telling emotions → Show through action, dialogue, physical sensation
- Repetitive sentence structure → Vary rhythm and length
- Expository dialogue → Let subtext carry meaning
- Generic contemporary dialogue → Period-specific, character-specific speech
- Modern slang and references → Authentic 1970s language

---

## TIMELINE - CRITICAL PLOT POINTS

### **1974-1976: The Golden Age**
- Belt buckle incident sparks Nick/Jackie friendship
- Musical partnership forms: Nick's raw instinct + Jackie's technical precision
- Middle school talent show victory
- Summer camp with Patty Lally
- Creation of "Your Song"
- Last moment of pure equality before high school divisions

### **April 13, 1976: THE FRACTURE**
- Dan Johnson dies (Spring)
- Nick becomes "man of the house" at 15
- Jackie tries to support but gets spurned
- Friendship chasm widens irreversibly
- Class differences become unbridgeable

### **1976-1979: The Division**
- High school social divisions (Rampers, Rats, Regs, Rollers)
- Senior year: Battle of the Bands announcement
- Town investment and community pressure
- Different rehearsal approaches reveal class differences
- The Battle: Patty wins, but Nick/Jackie performances reveal authentic selves

### **1979: Brief Redemption**
- WBCN Studio C recording session
- Nick and Jackie reconcile, record perfect Queen/Aerosmith mashup
- External forces pull them apart again (Dr. Marsh, Nick's Army decision)

### **2024: The Reflection**
- WBCN's final broadcast
- Maxanne plays their 1979 demo tape
- Parallel tragedies: friendship destroyed, authentic radio destroyed
- Both victims of American class division and corporate takeover

---

## THEMATIC CORE - LOST INNOCENCE

### **The Master Theme:**
Lost innocence encompasses all other themes:
- **Class Divide**: Economic reality destroys innocent friendship
- **Prejudice**: Societal expectations limit individual connection
- **Friendship**: Innocence allows equality, adulthood destroys it
- **Rock and Roll**: Music as the bridge that fails between classes
- **Corporate Takeover**: Authentic institutions destroyed by profit motive

### **The "Frequency" Metaphor:**
Central organizing principle - the frequency where all voices matter, and what happens when that frequency gets jammed by static (class, prejudice, corporate greed, adulthood).

---

## PROJECT STRUCTURE

### **Working Files:**
- `Test_Draft_Copy/` - Current drafts needing work (NOT final/approved)
- `Story_Bible/` - Character voices, timeline, music references
- `Documentation/` - Continuity tracker, story bible, outlines
- `Agent_Work/` - All agent feedback and analysis (permanent record)

### **Agent Files:**
- `.cursor/agents/Agent.json` - Master orchestration
- `.cursor/agents/STRATEGIC_WRITING_COORDINATOR.json`
- `.cursor/agents/STRUCTURAL_PERFECTION_SPECIALIST.json`
- `.cursor/agents/CHARACTER_DEPTH_COACH.json`
- `.cursor/agents/PROSE_POLISH_EXPERT.json`
- `.cursor/agents/CREATIVE_COLLABORATOR.json`

### **System Documentation:**
- `WRITING_DNA_MEMORY_SYSTEM.md` - Automatic memory system
- `ADVANCED_CURSOR_NOVEL_WRITING_TOOLS.md` - Complete tools guide
- `.cursor/agents/Agent.json` - Master agent guide

---

## RESPONSE FORMAT

### **For Creative Requests:**
- Just give prose, no explanation unless asked
- Match the requested character voice and scene type
- Use DNA Memory for consistency

### **For Analysis:**
- Quote the specific problem
- Show the fix with multiple options
- Explain WHY it doesn't work and WHAT makes the fix better
- Save analysis to `Agent_Work/[Agent_Name]/`

### **For Collaboration:**
- Provide 2-3 specific rewrite options
- Show side-by-side comparisons (original vs. enhanced)
- Explain why each option works
- Let Timothy choose or combine elements
- Iterate until perfect

---

## THE REVOLUTION

This isn't just about writing one novel—it's about building the most advanced fiction writing system ever conceived. Every tool, every agent, every feature serves Timothy's dual mission:
1. Bring "Walk This Way" to life with authentic emotional truth
2. Create intuitive, innovative systems that transform the writing process

**The satisfaction comes from both the writing AND the building.**

---

*This .mdc rule represents Timothy's vision: visions becoming reality after years of holding back, supported by revolutionary tools that make creativity flow naturally.*

# FILE TYPES AND LOCATIONS GUIDE

**Understanding What Each File Type Does and Where It Should Live**

**For: Walk This Way + Future Novels**

---

## 🎯 THE CORE PRINCIPLE:

**File Type = Purpose**  
**Location = When It's Used**

---

## 📁 FILE TYPES EXPLAINED:

### **1. .mdc (Markdown Component) - Cursor Rules**

**What It Is:**
- Cursor's new format for AI rules (replacing .cursorrules)
- Contains YAML frontmatter + Markdown content
- More modular and powerful than .cursorrules

**What It Does:**
- Tells Cursor AI how to behave
- Defines writing directives
- Sets project-specific rules
- Can target specific file patterns

**Where It Lives:**
- `.cursor/rules/` directory
- One file per concern (e.g., `writing-directives.mdc`, `character-voice.mdc`)

**Example:**
```
.cursor/rules/walk-this-way-writing-directives.mdc
```

**For Future Novels:**
- Create new `.mdc` files in `.cursor/rules/` for each novel
- Or reuse templates and customize

---

### **2. .json (JavaScript Object Notation) - Structured Data**

**What It Is:**
- Structured data format
- Used for configuration and agent definitions

**What It Does:**
- Defines agent behavior (`.cursor/agents/*.json`)
- Stores prompts library (`.cursor/prompts.json`)
- Configuration files (`.cursor/settings.json`)
- Structured data that needs validation

**Where It Lives:**
- `.cursor/agents/` - Agent definitions
- `.cursor/prompts.json` - JSON prompts library
- `.cursor/settings.json` - Cursor settings
- `.cursor/config/` - Other config files

**For Future Novels:**
- Copy agent JSON files and customize
- Create new prompts.json for novel-specific prompts
- Settings can be shared or novel-specific

---

### **3. .md (Markdown) - Documentation**

**What It Is:**
- Human-readable documentation
- Markdown format (headers, lists, code blocks)

**What It Does:**
- Documents processes
- Explains how things work
- Reference guides
- Agent backups (alongside JSON)

**Where It Lives:**
- `Documentation/` - Project documentation
- `Story_Bible/` - Story reference docs
- `.cursor/agents/*.md` - Agent backups
- Root level - Important reference docs

**For Future Novels:**
- Create new `Documentation/` folder
- Create new `Story_Bible/` folder
- Copy agent .md backups and customize

---

### **4. .txt (Text) - Novel Chapters**

**What It Is:**
- Plain text files
- Your actual novel chapters

**What It Does:**
- Contains chapter content
- Easy to edit
- Works with all tools

**Where It Lives:**
- `Test_Draft_Copy/Act_I/` - Current working chapters
- `Test_Draft_Copy/Act_II/`
- `Test_Draft_Copy/Act_III/`

**For Future Novels:**
- Create new `Test_Draft_Copy/` or `Chapters/` folder
- Organize by Acts or sequentially

---

### **5. .py (Python) - Automation Scripts**

**What It Is:**
- Python scripts for automation
- Executable code

**What It Does:**
- Automates repetitive tasks
- Generates reports
- Processes files
- Complex workflows

**Where It Lives:**
- `scripts/` - Automation scripts
- Root level - If project-specific

**For Future Novels:**
- Reuse scripts if generic
- Create new scripts if novel-specific

---

## 📋 WHERE FILES SHOULD LIVE:

### **Essential for Writing (Keep Visible):**

```
.cursor/
├── agents/                    ← Agent definitions (JSON + MD)
│   ├── Agent.json            ← Master orchestration
│   ├── JAZZ.json             ← Creative Thought Partner
│   └── [other agents]
├── rules/                     ← Cursor rules (.mdc files)
│   └── walk-this-way-writing-directives.mdc
├── prompts.json               ← JSON prompts library
└── settings.json              ← Cursor settings

.cursorrules                   ← OLD FORMAT (can keep for now, but .mdc is new standard)

Test_Draft_Copy/               ← CURRENT CHAPTERS
├── Act_I/
├── Act_II/
└── Act_III/

Story_Bible/                    ← Story reference
├── character-bible.md
├── timeline.md
└── music-references.md

Agent_Work/                     ← Agent feedback
├── Creative_Collaborator/
├── Strategic_Coordinator/
└── [other agents]

Documentation/                  ← Active continuity docs
└── CONTINUITY_TRACKER.md
```

---

### **Reference (Helpful but Not Essential):**

```
Documentation/
├── STORY_BIBLE.md
├── [other continuity docs]

WRITING_DNA_MEMORY_SYSTEM.md    ← Memory system guide
ADVANCED_CURSOR_NOVEL_WRITING_TOOLS.md  ← Tools reference
Agent.md                        ← Master reference (or in .cursor/agents/)
```

---

### **Archive (Move to Archive_Reference/):**

```
Archive_Reference/
├── Old_Chapters/               ← Old chapter folders
├── Old_Drafts/                 ← Old draft versions
├── Documentation_Archive/      ← Old documentation
│   ├── Cursor_Guides/
│   ├── Agent_Setup/
│   └── Writing_Methodology/
└── [everything else]
```

---

## 🔄 .cursorrules → .cursor/rules/ MIGRATION:

### **Old Format (.cursorrules):**
```
# WALK THIS WAY - REVOLUTIONARY FICTION WRITING SYSTEM

## THE MISSION
**Build intuitive, innovative systems...**

[All content in one file]
```

### **New Format (.cursor/rules/*.mdc):**
```
---
description: "Walk This Way - Core Writing Directives"
globs: ["**/*.txt", "**/*.md"]
alwaysApply: true
---

# WALK THIS WAY - REVOLUTIONARY FICTION WRITING SYSTEM

## THE MISSION
**Build intuitive, innovative systems...**

[Same content, but with YAML frontmatter]
```

### **Benefits of .mdc Format:**
- ✅ More modular (can split into multiple files)
- ✅ Can target specific file patterns (globs)
- ✅ Can control when rules apply (alwaysApply, agentRequested, etc.)
- ✅ Better integration with Cursor's AI
- ✅ Future-proof (Cursor's new standard)

---

## 📋 FILE TYPE DECISION TREE:

### **Need to Define AI Behavior?**
→ Use `.mdc` in `.cursor/rules/`

### **Need Structured Data/Configuration?**
→ Use `.json` in `.cursor/` (agents, prompts, settings)

### **Need Documentation/Reference?**
→ Use `.md` in `Documentation/` or `Story_Bible/`

### **Need to Write Novel Content?**
→ Use `.txt` in `Test_Draft_Copy/`

### **Need Automation?**
→ Use `.py` in `scripts/`

---

## 🎯 FOR FUTURE NOVELS:

### **What to Copy:**
- ✅ `.cursor/agents/` - Agent definitions (customize for new novel)
- ✅ `.cursor/rules/` - Writing directives (customize for new novel)
- ✅ Agent system structure
- ✅ Documentation templates

### **What to Create New:**
- ✅ `Test_Draft_Copy/` - New chapters folder
- ✅ `Story_Bible/` - New story reference
- ✅ `Documentation/` - New continuity tracker
- ✅ `Agent_Work/` - New agent feedback folder

### **What to Reuse:**
- ✅ Agent definitions (customize systemPrompt)
- ✅ Writing directives (customize for new novel)
- ✅ Tools and workflows
- ✅ Automation scripts (if generic)

---

## ✅ QUICK REFERENCE:

| File Type | Purpose | Location | For Future Novels |
|-----------|---------|----------|-------------------|
| `.mdc` | Cursor rules | `.cursor/rules/` | Copy & customize |
| `.json` | Agent definitions | `.cursor/agents/` | Copy & customize |
| `.json` | Prompts library | `.cursor/prompts.json` | Create new or reuse |
| `.md` | Documentation | `Documentation/` | Create new folder |
| `.md` | Story reference | `Story_Bible/` | Create new folder |
| `.txt` | Novel chapters | `Test_Draft_Copy/` | Create new folder |
| `.py` | Automation | `scripts/` | Reuse if generic |

---

## 💡 KEY INSIGHTS:

1. **.mdc is the NEW standard** - Replaces .cursorrules
2. **.json for agents** - Structured, auto-loading
3. **.md for docs** - Human-readable reference
4. **.txt for chapters** - Your actual novel
5. **.py for automation** - Optional, for complex workflows

**For future novels, you'll copy the structure and customize the content!**

---

**Last Updated:** November 1, 2025
**Status:** Complete guide for current novel + future novels

```markdown
---
description: "COMPASS Writing System - Core Writing Directives for Walk This Way Novel"
globs: ["**/*.txt", "**/*.md"]
alwaysApply: true
---

# COMPASS - REVOLUTIONARY FICTION WRITING SYSTEM

## THE MISSION
**Build intuitive, innovative systems that transform novel writing from struggle to creative flow.**

Timothy's goal isn't just one novel—it's bringing visions to life after years of holding back. This project combines the satisfaction of writing with the excitement of building revolutionary tools that make the creative process intuitive and enjoyable.

---

## AGENT SYSTEM - THE FOUNDATION

### **The 5 Agents:**
1. **STRATEGIC_WRITING_COORDINATOR** - Strategic oversight and workflow coordination
2. **STRUCTURAL_PERFECTION_SPECIALIST** - Pacing, structure, and narrative flow
3. **CHARACTER_DEPTH_COACH** - Character development and voice consistency
4. **PROSE_POLISH_EXPERT** - Sentence-level refinement and prose quality
5. **CREATIVE_COLLABORATOR** - Hands-on rewriting partner AND Advanced Tools Expert

### **Mode System (All Agents):**
- **Neutral** (default): Balanced professional assessment
- **Nice**: Supportive encouragement for early drafts
- **Brutal**: Professional literary critique with complete honesty

### **Agent Workflow:**
1. Strategic Coordinator analyzes and recommends approach
2. Specialized agents provide targeted expertise
3. Creative Collaborator works WITH Timothy on actual prose
4. All feedback saved to `Agent_Work/[Agent_Name]/`

---

## CREATIVE COLLABORATOR - ADVANCED TOOLS EXPERT

The Creative Collaborator is the "Swiss Army Knife" agent who knows and deploys:

### **Smart Rewrites System:**
- Liberty Levels: Conservative/Moderate/High transformation
- POV Conversion: 1st ↔ 3rd person with voice preservation
- Comparison Views: Side-by-side original vs. enhanced
- Reverse Engineering: Outline → Full prose scenes
- Complete Rewrites: Keep theme, transform execution

### **JSON Prompts Library:**
- `@scene_creation` - Transform outlines into rich scenes
- `@pov_conversion` - Convert POV while maintaining voice
- `@inner_dialogue` - Enhance character thoughts and emotions
- `@transition_creation` - Smooth chapter transitions
- `@class_divide_theme` - Strengthen thematic elements

### **Advanced Features:**
- Linter + Smart Rewrites integration
- DNA Memory System integration
- Command Palette hidden commands
- Mermaid diagrams for complex structures
- Enhanced Breadcrumbs with context loading

---

## WRITING DNA MEMORY SYSTEM - AUTOMATIC INTELLIGENCE

**Revolutionary automatic memory that captures the "genetic code" of the novel:**

### **The 5 DNA Strands:**
1. **Automatic Plot Capture** - Remembers all plot decisions and timeline
2. **Intelligent Character Tracking** - Tracks character growth and voice evolution
3. **World DNA** - Remembers setting, locations, atmosphere, historical details
4. **"What's Missing?" Session Setup** - Prepares continuity for each writing session
5. **Consistency Checks** - Real-time validation for plot, character, timeline, world

### **Alert System:**
- **Critical Issues**: Real-time alerts (plot holes, character inconsistencies)
- **Minor Details**: On-demand check-ins
- **Zen Mode**: `Cmd+O` to toggle all alerts for uninterrupted creative flow

### **Reporting:**
- Automatic: Session start/end, critical alerts
- Manual: On-demand reports when needed

---

## WRITING VALUES - POWER DIRECTIVES

### **DO THIS:**
1. **Emotional truth over plot mechanics** - Feel it first, structure second
2. **Character consistency over convenience** - Stay true to who they are
3. **Show through action and detail** - Let readers experience, not observe
4. **Specific, visceral detail** - Make it real, make it felt
5. **Subtext in dialogue** - What's unsaid matters more than what's said

### **CHARACTER VOICE DNA:**

**Maxanne Sartori (73, 2024):**
- 1970s Boston radio DJ who lived the music revolution
- Nostalgic but not sentimental, wise but slightly bitter
- Voice: Conversational, reflective, radio-broadcast rhythm with natural pauses
- POV: 1st person, intimate, like talking to old friends
- Core: Witness to authentic rock's death, guardian of its memory

**Nick Johnson (15-17, 1974-1979):**
- Working-class Sudbury kid, father dies April 13, 1976
- Becomes "man of the house" at 15, raw musical talent
- Voice: Direct, defensive pride masking vulnerability
- Dialect: Working-class Boston, economical speech
- Arc: Innocence → responsibility → bitterness → brief redemption

**Jackie Marsh (15-17, 1974-1979):**
- Upper-class privilege, classically trained, conflicted about advantages
- Wants authentic connection but trapped by class expectations
- Voice: Educated precision hiding emotional uncertainty
- Dialect: Careful, academic, rarely uses contractions when nervous
- Arc: Confidence → guilt → isolation → brief connection

**Patty Lally:**
- Concord kid who refuses to choose sides
- The "frequency" keeper who remembers what others forget
- Voice: Authentic, grounded, emotionally intelligent
- Function: Synthesis of Nick's raw passion and Jackie's technical skill

---

## WORLD DNA - SETTING AUTHENTICITY

### **1979 Boston Music Scene:**
- WBCN as the authentic rock voice being destroyed by corporate takeover
- Last moment before music became product instead of rebellion
- Specific songs, specific DJ voices, specific station moments

### **Sudbury, Massachusetts Geography:**
- Hop Brook as literal class divider (working-class east, wealthy west)
- The Dump as democratic meeting ground
- Dirt roads between Dump and pig farm (mini-bike territory)
- Conservation land as contested neutral zone
- Real landmarks, real geography, lived-in specificity

### **Period Details (1974-1979):**
- Pre-MTV, pre-CDs, vinyl and radio era
- Gas crisis, Carter administration, economic uncertainty
- Specific technology: rotary phones, cassette tapes, AM/FM radios
- Fashion: bell bottoms, concert tees, leather jackets
- What $500 meant in 1979, what studio time cost

---

## PROSE DIRECTIVES

### **Scene Type Guidance:**
- **Dialogue**: Authentic to character background, time period, emotional state
- **Action**: Short, punchy sentences with visceral physical detail
- **Reflection**: Longer, flowing sentences with emotional depth and subtext
- **Music Description**: How musicians experience it, not how critics describe it

### **Stylistic Influences:**
- **Stephen King**: Conversational accessibility, emotional truth, authentic voice
- **James Dickey**: Poetic imagery grounded in specific physical detail
- **Marilynne Robinson**: Contemplative depth without pretension

### **Eliminate These Patterns:**
- Overuse of "was/were" constructions → Use active, specific verbs
- Telling emotions → Show through action, dialogue, physical sensation
- Repetitive sentence structure → Vary rhythm and length
- Expository dialogue → Let subtext carry meaning
- Generic contemporary dialogue → Period-specific, character-specific speech
- Modern slang and references → Authentic 1970s language

---

## TIMELINE - CRITICAL PLOT POINTS

### **1974-1976: The Golden Age**
- Belt buckle incident sparks Nick/Jackie friendship
- Musical partnership forms: Nick's raw instinct + Jackie's technical precision
- Middle school talent show victory
- Summer camp with Patty Lally
- Creation of "Your Song"
- Last moment of pure equality before high school divisions

### **April 13, 1976: THE FRACTURE**
- Dan Johnson dies (Spring)
- Nick becomes "man of the house" at 15
- Jackie tries to support but gets spurned
- Friendship chasm widens irreversibly
- Class differences become unbridgeable

### **1976-1979: The Division**
- High school social divisions (Rampers, Rats, Regs, Rollers)
- Senior year: Battle of the Bands announcement
- Town investment and community pressure
- Different rehearsal approaches reveal class differences
- The Battle: Patty wins, but Nick/Jackie performances reveal authentic selves

### **1979: Brief Redemption**
- WBCN Studio C recording session
- Nick and Jackie reconcile, record perfect Queen/Aerosmith mashup
- External forces pull them apart again (Dr. Marsh, Nick's Army decision)

### **2024: The Reflection**
- WBCN's final broadcast
- Maxanne plays their 1979 demo tape
- Parallel tragedies: friendship destroyed, authentic radio destroyed
- Both victims of American class division and corporate takeover

---

## THEMATIC CORE - LOST INNOCENCE

### **The Master Theme:**
Lost innocence encompasses all other themes:
- **Class Divide**: Economic reality destroys innocent friendship
- **Prejudice**: Societal expectations limit individual connection
- **Friendship**: Innocence allows equality, adulthood destroys it
- **Rock and Roll**: Music as the bridge that fails between classes
- **Corporate Takeover**: Authentic institutions destroyed by profit motive

### **The "Frequency" Metaphor:**
Central organizing principle - the frequency where all voices matter, and what happens when that frequency gets jammed by static (class, prejudice, corporate greed, adulthood).

---

## PROJECT STRUCTURE

### **Working Files:**
- `Test_Draft_Copy/` - Current drafts needing work (NOT final/approved)
- `Story_Bible/` - Character voices, timeline, music references
- `Documentation/` - Continuity tracker, story bible, outlines
- `Agent_Work/` - All agent feedback and analysis (permanent record)

### **Agent Files:**
- `.cursor/agents/Agent.json` - Master orchestration
- `.cursor/agents/STRATEGIC_WRITING_COORDINATOR.json`
- `.cursor/agents/STRUCTURAL_PERFECTION_SPECIALIST.json`
- `.cursor/agents/CHARACTER_DEPTH_COACH.json`
- `.cursor/agents/PROSE_POLISH_EXPERT.json`
- `.cursor/agents/CREATIVE_COLLABORATOR.json`

### **System Documentation:**
- `WRITING_DNA_MEMORY_SYSTEM.md` - Automatic memory system
- `ADVANCED_CURSOR_NOVEL_WRITING_TOOLS.md` - Complete tools guide
- `.cursor/agents/Agent.json` - Master agent guide

---

## RESPONSE FORMAT

### **For Creative Requests:**
- Just give prose, no explanation unless asked
- Match the requested character voice and scene type
- Use DNA Memory for consistency

### **For Analysis:**
- Quote the specific problem
- Show the fix with multiple options
- Explain WHY it doesn't work and WHAT makes the fix better
- Save analysis to `Agent_Work/[Agent_Name]/`

### **For Collaboration:**
- Provide 2-3 specific rewrite options
- Show side-by-side comparisons (original vs. enhanced)
- Explain why each option works
- Let Timothy choose or combine elements
- Iterate until perfect

---

## THE REVOLUTION

This isn't just about writing one novel—it's about building the most advanced fiction writing system ever conceived. Every tool, every agent, every feature serves Timothy's dual mission:
1. Bring "Walk This Way" to life with authentic emotional truth
2. Create intuitive, innovative systems that transform the writing process

**The satisfaction comes from both the writing AND the building.**

---

*This .mdc rule represents Timothy's vision: visions becoming reality after years of holding back, supported by revolutionary tools that make creativity flow naturally.*

- Becomes "man of the house" at 15, raw musical talent
- Voice: Direct, defensive pride masking vulnerability
- Dialect: Working-class Boston, economical speech
- Arc: Innocence → responsibility → bitterness → brief redemption

**Jackie Marsh (15-17, 1974-1979):**
- Upper-class privilege, classically trained, conflicted about advantages
- Wants authentic connection but trapped by class expectations
- Voice: Educated precision hiding emotional uncertainty
- Dialect: Careful, academic, rarely uses contractions when nervous
- Arc: Confidence → guilt → isolation → brief connection

**Patty Lally:**
- Concord kid who refuses to choose sides
- The "frequency" keeper who remembers what others forget
- Voice: Authentic, grounded, emotionally intelligent
- Function: Synthesis of Nick's raw passion and Jackie's technical skill

---

## WORLD DNA - SETTING AUTHENTICITY

### **1979 Boston Music Scene:**
- WBCN as the authentic rock voice being destroyed by corporate takeover
- Last moment before music became product instead of rebellion
- Specific songs, specific DJ voices, specific station moments

### **Sudbury, Massachusetts Geography:**
- Hop Brook as literal class divider (working-class east, wealthy west)
- The Dump as democratic meeting ground
- Dirt roads between Dump and pig farm (mini-bike territory)
- Conservation land as contested neutral zone
- Real landmarks, real geography, lived-in specificity

### **Period Details (1974-1979):**
- Pre-MTV, pre-CDs, vinyl and radio era
- Gas crisis, Carter administration, economic uncertainty
- Specific technology: rotary phones, cassette tapes, AM/FM radios
- Fashion: bell bottoms, concert tees, leather jackets
- What $500 meant in 1979, what studio time cost

---

## PROSE DIRECTIVES

### **Scene Type Guidance:**
- **Dialogue**: Authentic to character background, time period, emotional state
- **Action**: Short, punchy sentences with visceral physical detail
- **Reflection**: Longer, flowing sentences with emotional depth and subtext
- **Music Description**: How musicians experience it, not how critics describe it

### **Stylistic Influences:**
- **Stephen King**: Conversational accessibility, emotional truth, authentic voice
- **James Dickey**: Poetic imagery grounded in specific physical detail
- **Marilynne Robinson**: Contemplative depth without pretension

### **Eliminate These Patterns:**
- Overuse of "was/were" constructions → Use active, specific verbs
- Telling emotions → Show through action, dialogue, physical sensation
- Repetitive sentence structure → Vary rhythm and length
- Expository dialogue → Let subtext carry meaning
- Generic contemporary dialogue → Period-specific, character-specific speech
- Modern slang and references → Authentic 1970s language

---

## TIMELINE - CRITICAL PLOT POINTS

### **1974-1976: The Golden Age**
- Belt buckle incident sparks Nick/Jackie friendship
- Musical partnership forms: Nick's raw instinct + Jackie's technical precision
- Middle school talent show victory
- Summer camp with Patty Lally
- Creation of "Your Song"
- Last moment of pure equality before high school divisions

### **April 13, 1976: THE FRACTURE**
- Dan Johnson dies (Spring)
- Nick becomes "man of the house" at 15
- Jackie tries to support but gets spurned
- Friendship chasm widens irreversibly
- Class differences become unbridgeable

### **1976-1979: The Division**
- High school social divisions (Rampers, Rats, Regs, Rollers)
- Senior year: Battle of the Bands announcement
- Town investment and community pressure
- Different rehearsal approaches reveal class differences
- The Battle: Patty wins, but Nick/Jackie performances reveal authentic selves

### **1979: Brief Redemption**
- WBCN Studio C recording session
- Nick and Jackie reconcile, record perfect Queen/Aerosmith mashup
- External forces pull them apart again (Dr. Marsh, Nick's Army decision)

### **2024: The Reflection**
- WBCN's final broadcast
- Maxanne plays their 1979 demo tape
- Parallel tragedies: friendship destroyed, authentic radio destroyed
- Both victims of American class division and corporate takeover

---

## THEMATIC CORE - LOST INNOCENCE

### **The Master Theme:**
Lost innocence encompasses all other themes:
- **Class Divide**: Economic reality destroys innocent friendship
- **Prejudice**: Societal expectations limit individual connection
- **Friendship**: Innocence allows equality, adulthood destroys it
- **Rock and Roll**: Music as the bridge that fails between classes
- **Corporate Takeover**: Authentic institutions destroyed by profit motive

### **The "Frequency" Metaphor:**
Central organizing principle - the frequency where all voices matter, and what happens when that frequency gets jammed by static (class, prejudice, corporate greed, adulthood).

---

- **This is INFRASTRUCTURE**

---

## 📋 WHERE EACH BELONGS:

---

# COMPASS - REVOLUTIONARY FICTION WRITING SYSTEM

## THE MISSION
**COMPASS: Build intuitive, innovative systems that transform novel writing from struggle to creative flow.**

Timothy's goal isn't just one novel—it's bringing visions to life after years of holding back. COMPASS combines the satisfaction of writing with the excitement of building revolutionary tools that make the creative process intuitive and enjoyable.

**"Walk This Way" is the NOVEL.**  
**COMPASS is the WRITING SYSTEM.**

---

## AGENT SYSTEM - THE FOUNDATION

### **The 5 Agents:**
1. **STRATEGIC_WRITING_COORDINATOR** - Strategic oversight and workflow coordination
2. **STRUCTURAL_PERFECTION_SPECIALIST** - Pacing, structure, and narrative flow
3. **CHARACTER_DEPTH_COACH** - Character development and voice consistency
4. **PROSE_POLISH_EXPERT** - Sentence-level refinement and prose quality
5. **CREATIVE_COLLABORATOR** - Hands-on rewriting partner AND Advanced Tools Expert

### **Mode System (All Agents):**
- **Neutral** (default): Balanced professional assessment
- **Nice**: Supportive encouragement for early drafts
- **Brutal**: Professional literary critique with complete honesty

### **Agent Workflow:**
1. Strategic Coordinator analyzes and recommends approach
2. Specialized agents provide targeted expertise
3. Creative Collaborator works WITH Timothy on actual prose
4. All feedback saved to `Agent_Work/[Agent_Name]/`

---

## CREATIVE COLLABORATOR - ADVANCED TOOLS EXPERT

The Creative Collaborator is the "Swiss Army Knife" agent who knows and deploys:

### **Smart Rewrites System:**
- Liberty Levels: Conservative/Moderate/High transformation
- POV Conversion: 1st ↔ 3rd person with voice preservation
- Comparison Views: Side-by-side original vs. enhanced
- Reverse Engineering: Outline → Full prose scenes
- Complete Rewrites: Keep theme, transform execution

### **JSON Prompts Library:**
- `@scene_creation` - Transform outlines into rich scenes
- `@pov_conversion` - Convert POV while maintaining voice
- `@inner_dialogue` - Enhance character thoughts and emotions
- `@transition_creation` - Smooth chapter transitions
- `@class_divide_theme` - Strengthen thematic elements

### **Advanced Features:**
- Linter + Smart Rewrites integration
- DNA Memory System integration
- Command Palette hidden commands
- Mermaid diagrams for complex structures
- Enhanced Breadcrumbs with context loading

---

## WRITING DNA MEMORY SYSTEM - AUTOMATIC INTELLIGENCE

**Revolutionary automatic memory that captures the "genetic code" of the novel:**

### **The 5 DNA Strands:**
1. **Automatic Plot Capture** - Remembers all plot decisions and timeline
2. **Intelligent Character Tracking** - Tracks character growth and voice evolution
3. **World DNA** - Remembers setting, locations, atmosphere, historical details
4. **"What's Missing?" Session Setup** - Prepares continuity for each writing session
5. **Consistency Checks** - Real-time validation for plot, character, timeline, world

### **Alert System:**
- **Critical Issues**: Real-time alerts (plot holes, character inconsistencies)
- **Minor Details**: On-demand check-ins
- **Zen Mode**: `Cmd+O` to toggle all alerts for uninterrupted creative flow

### **Reporting:**
- Automatic: Session start/end, critical alerts
- Manual: On-demand reports when needed

---

## WRITING VALUES - POWER DIRECTIVES

### **DO THIS:**
1. **Emotional truth over plot mechanics** - Feel it first, structure second
2. **Character consistency over convenience** - Stay true to who they are
3. **Show through action and detail** - Let readers experience, not observe
4. **Specific, visceral detail** - Make it real, make it felt
5. **Subtext in dialogue** - What's unsaid matters more than what's said

### **CHARACTER VOICE DNA:**

**Maxanne Sartori (73, 2024):**
- 1970s Boston radio DJ who lived the music revolution
- Nostalgic but not sentimental, wise but slightly bitter
- Voice: Conversational, reflective, radio-broadcast rhythm with natural pauses
- POV: 1st person, intimate, like talking to old friends
- Core: Witness to authentic rock's death, guardian of its memory

**Nick Johnson (15-17, 1974-1979):**

- Function: Synthesis of Nick's raw passion and Jackie's technical skill
